[TRANSLATION]
1     A certain Isidoros, son of Artemidoros,
2     an Alexandrian, submitted a petition to Ulpius Marcellus, the
3     then dikaiodotes acting as deputy
4     at that time also for the matters pertaining to the administration,
5     wishing to lease for 2 years
6     from the 7th year of Antoninus
7     Caesar the lord and of the god Verus,
8     around the field of Magdola in the toparchy
9     of the Thmoiribite, from the account (of unproductive land)
10    … the plots, of arouras 36 … —ran
11    … on condition that he sow the afore… , [to whom] if
12    he choose, with crops, and pay per aroura
13    [drachmas] … ; and Marcellus wrote
14    [on the …] of the month Tybi of the 7th year to Horion,
15    the former strategos, having sent also of the
16    petitions submitted to him
17    the other one, so that if the said
18    land should fall under lease, by public proclamation … of the better
19    bid. And of Horion the things written
20    … —ntos
21    so that I may know and fill up my own portions, I reckoned the
22    rent gathering according to
23    the acceptance-offer, and I passed it on for public proclamation. And the time being fulfilled into
24    the 8th year, the village-scribe
25    at the inspection reported
26    that the arouras accepted for
27    lease were in dry (uncultivated) land, unclaimed, and that the man who
28    made the undertaking was unknown; and those who inspected the current-year
29    crops of the same 8th year
30    wrote that it appeared to them to be
31    in dry land, from which nothing could
32    accrue. And in the 9th year the same
33    village-scribe, declaring the same things as those reported in the 8th year
34    and setting beside (them) the things written by those
35    who inspected in the 8th year, remitted the rent taken up from the undertaking;
36    for the time of the acceptance-offer,
37    he says, was fulfilled into the 8th year,
38    and those who inspected had written
39    … to be in dry land,
40    from which nothing could accrue.
41    Wherefore accordingly the sum-total (assessed) by the village-scribe
42    … in the 9th year and on behalf
43    of the 10th year was passed on to be held over until
44    judgment; wherefore also for the 11th year it is held over.
45    Of the Thmoiribite, Magdola:
46    of dry unproductive land previously passed on,
47    … of the total as rent of pastures
48    … 1800 drachmas, of additional charges 112 drachmas 3 obols, total 1912 drachmas 3 obols,
49    [sum-totals] from the underlying (assessments) and others
50    [taken up among] those from the village of Nemeo,
51    [the village-scribe of the] 9th year, saying that the village,
52    [when the sum-totals were] established to be given, was
53    [of men] 150, and that there had been imposed
54    [on them of wheat] 100 artabas, and in addition … now had come down to only 45 men, of whom
55    [the 34 who had grown weak] had withdrawn,
56    [and only 11] remained, remitted the (amount) falling to the withdrawn
57    and added that Annius Syriacus,
58    the former prefect, in like cases
59    had judged in the 3rd year, when he was reckoning the nome,
60    that those remaining
61    should pay the (amount) falling to them. Whatever therefore
62    of these falls to those declared
63    to remain, (is) to be exacted,
64    and the (amounts) remaining after this
65    were passed on to be held over until judgment in the 9th year and the 10th year;
66    wherefore also for the 11th year in the same
67    manner it is held over.
68    Of the Phernouphite, Nemeo, of the administration:
69    of the underlying (dues) of the village-scribe's travel-allowance
70    of the reckoning, 50 drachmas;
71    of the topogrammateus's travel-allowance of the reckoning, 50 drachmas;
72    total of underlying (dues) 100 drachmas, which net 94 drachmas ½-obol 2 chalkoi,
73    of additional charges 5 drachmas 5 obols 2 chalkoi, total the same,

77    of additional charges 50 drachmas 4 obols ½ 1 chalkous, total 860 drachmas 1 obol ½ 3 chalkoi,

79    of additional charges 3 obols ½, total [9 drachmas] 4 obols ½,
80    total for the same 870 drachmas 3 chalkoi,

166   Certain village-scribes remitted sum-totals from the underlying (dues) and others declared to be taken up among the men registered in the villages,
167   on the ground that in olden times, when, they say,
168   the sum-totals were established to be given, the villages were populous, but now
169   some had failed entirely, and others
170   from a larger number of men
171   had come down to few,
172   of whom the greater part, having grown weak,
173   had withdrawn; and they added
174   that Bassaeus Rufus, the most illustrious,
175   in the 9th year, when he reckoned the nome,
176   had released the like on other villages
177   of the nome. The (amounts) therefore
178   remitted by them, as next
179   set out village by village, in the 9th year,
180   and likewise also on behalf of the 10th year, were held over
181   until the decision of the most excellent prefect;
182   wherefore also for the 11th year
183   it is held over.

1821  And of those of the 22nd year:
1822  Of grain-bearing private fully-registered (lands), the one was assigned among the unsold,
1823  the other was ceded, and of the
1824  remainder, held in possession, the price was paid.
1825  And the (land) among the unsold, in dry land
1826  having been inspected, the (dues) falling
1827  to it from the ceded (land),
1828  being under exaction,
1829  were assigned in the 22nd year and thereafter year by year
1830  until the 9th year, and in the 10th year, of some
1831  of them the payments on reasonable
1832  causes were restored to agreement;
1833  wherefore also for the 11th year the money-payments,
1834  the grain-payments in another section having been assigned,
1835  are placed, since as is set out
1836  nothing as concerning them judged
1837  became manifest to me.
1838  Of the Psaneite, Sao, of Sarapion, the (son)
1839  of Achilleus, and of Didymos also called Demetrios, of the administration arouras 60 ¾ 8 16 32 64,

1841  account (of unproductive land) arouras 26 8 16 64, of dry-vine (land) likewise

1844  twentieth 127 drachmas 2 obols ½ 2 chalkoi, of additional charges 7 drachmas 5 obols ½ 2 chalkoi,
1845  total 135 drachmas 2 obols ½,
1846  of naubion equal (arouras), of which the whole by the (scribe) before me,
1847  royal scribe, on behalf of the 6th year was assigned, arouras 63 ¾
1848  of silver 31 drachmas 5 obols 2 chalkoi,
1849  of embankment-tax … 127 ½ chalkoi which (are) 2 obols 2 chalkoi, of exchange 3 drachmas 2 obols 3 chalkoi,
1850  total 35 drachmas 3 obols ½ 3 chalkoi, of additional charges 2 obols 3 chalkoi, total 37 drachmas [5 obols] 2 chalkoi,
1851  of treasury-dues 4 obols 3 chalkoi, of freightage of wheat artabas 196 2/3 12
1852  of silver 78 drachmas 4 obols ½, total for the same 252 drachmas 4 obols ½ 1 chalkous
1853  … in the 22nd year from Pos[tum—] … then at the idios logos
1854  … and of the ensuing
1855  [inquiry] it appeared that Petemious
1856  [the former village]-scribe of Psenacho, through a report which he lodged
1857  [in the] local public
1858  [archive], informing on 54 men
1859  … as not at all registered
1860  … but as falsely
1861  … having been shown. And then
1862  indeed, according to the information, the poll-taxes of the necessary years are declared to have been reckoned. But the (scribe) after Petemious
1863  appointed as village-scribe declared
1864  that some of them, upon the
1865  informed-upon villages,
1866  did not exist nor had ever existed
1867  but that it was even unknown who they are,
1868  and that others were registered upon Thmouis and Mendes, and others
1869  were poll-taxed upon the informed-upon villages, having come in here for the first time as though from abroad
1870  in the 23rd year. Whence the poll-taxes of the one
1871  were let go under exaction,
1872  and those of the others were held over. And Victorinus, in relation to what had been reported
1873  to him, wishing an inquiry
1874  of all to be made, it was brought
1875  to Syriacus in the 3rd year, when the nome
1876  he was reckoning, concerning those assigned until the 2nd year,
1877  and he subscribed thus:
1878  "Concerning those registered in the metropolis,
1879  within three months let it be inquired
1880  whether they retain that right,
1881  and of the rest let it be remitted." Accordingly
1882  the poll-taxes of the others
1883  are declared to have been remitted, and those of those declared
1884  to be registered in the metropolis until the 5th year
1885  were assigned and thereafter year by year; wherefore
1886  also for the 11th year it is placed.
